SOLD AT A RECORD PRICE BY LE HUYNH
106 + 108 JOHN ST, CABRAMATTA
TWO PROPERTIES MUST BE SOLD IN ONE LINE
These two adjoining properties are located in the middle of Cabramatta CBD and just a short walk to Cabramatta Station.
Zone R4 development site
Total land size approximately 1,374m2 (30.06m by 45.72m)

HOUSE 106 JOHN STREET
Double storey cladding hous
4 bedrooms
2 bathrooms
Living room. Kitchen. Laundry.

HOUSE 108 JOHN STREET
Double storey brick house
5 bedrooms with built in robes
Master bedroom with en suite
Main bathroom with separate toilet
Total 3 bathrooms and 3 toilet
Large living area opening to a large balcony.
Combine kitchen and dining with a balcony faced to the back yard
Timber flooring throughout
Spacious double garage

Come have lunch or dinner at the restaurants in Cabramatta. You will soon discover the reason why this suburb is known as 'Little Vietnam', many of the signs are written in Vietnamese. So please come enjoy a bowl of pho, Bún bò Huế or a bun from the bakery as well as 'fried banana'. South East Asian cuisine can be shown throughout the whole business district from John Street, to Park Road, Hughes Street, Hill Street and Railway Parade.
From the oriental lamp shades to the Medibank stall, would make someone wonder, ‘Where am I?’. Welcome and see the joy that is on offer as Cabramatta is open for business!
The residence is in a notable location, close to Cabramatta CBD. Marked by the stone statues of the mother pig with her piglets on ‘John Street’, demonstrates the vision from the local community to create a better future for their families. The tortoise sculpture becomes a climbable perch for the children as well as the white ‘pailau’ offers a sight to behold with a marble foundation as well as the ornate details. Can you see the Australian native animals etched into the woodwork on the gate? (If you look carefully find the kangaroo and the koala).
Lion Dancing is commonly displayed during the Moon Festival, and Lunar New Year. Fireworks, will be incredibly close to where you will be living, afterwards it will be within walking distance back home. The fireworks are shown in the evening to help you finish off the festivities after a long day of eating through the food stalls and watching the shows on offer.
'Tony's Bakery' has been serving pork rolls for decades and became a household name long ago. Amongst the wonderful selection of bubble tea shops from 'Milk Flower','Sharetea', 'Machi Machi' and 'My Modern', has generated a family friendly atmosphere for the local community to enjoy. The morning rush, through to lunch as well as dinner sees a variety of different patrons passing through the arcades, the food court of BKK as well as the Dutton Lane Shopping Centre. The local community so often says that Cabramatta rarely ever closes, as it has so much to offer.

*** All offers must be in writing ***

SOLD BY BASEL NAHAS & KEVIN HILL
4/23 MEACHER STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
Set within a boutique complex of just eight townhouses, this free-standing townhouse resembles house-like proportions with its dual level design and perfect for the first home buyer and investors alike. With a rental potential of approx. $700 per week, it is conveniently located within walking distance of Mount Druitt Westfield, Mount Druitt Station, vibrant rooftop dining and places of worship.

- Open-plan lounge and dining area, seamlessly flowing onto outdoor courtyard
- Four bedrooms, all with built-in mirrored wardrobes, including a master with ensuite
- Modern gas kitchen is equipped with steel appliances, dishwasher and cupboard space
- Readymade family home or investment, potential to enhance and add your personal touches
- Single lock up garage with internal access plus an additional car space for extra parking
- Boutique complex with visitor car spaces and a playground for kids to enjoy outdoors
- Well-appointed main bathroom, tiled with a separate bath and shower, internal laundry
- Air-conditioning and down-lighting, neutral blend of polished tiles and floorboards
- Minutes from Mount Druitt Hospital, Westfield Mount Druitt and local amenities

Council rates: $456 per quarter
Strata rates: $453 per quarter
Water rates: $200 per quarter

SOLD BY BASEL NAHAS & KEVIN HILL
11/30 HYTHE STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
Conveniently set metres from the buzz of Mount Druitt's vibrant CBD, this beautifully refreshed two-bedroom apartment has been exceptionally transformed to create a modern living environment suited to an astute professional or first home buyer. It offers a streamlined, open plan design with high quality hybrid floors, a contemporary style kitchen and a private leafy balcony. The property is placed within an established building and located just 900m from Station, Westfield and an array of dining options.

- Modern interiors unveil a free-flowing lounge/dining area with quality hybrid flooring
- Seamless in/outdoor flow to the serene outdoor space, relaxed all-weather balcony
- Well-maintained kitchen features stainless steel gas appliances, wide breakfast bar
- Two generous bedrooms offer highlight windows, both fitted with built-in wardrobes
- Predominately tiled bathroom includes a soaking tub and shower, internal laundry
- Air-conditioning, ceiling fans, neutral colour scheme, elegant style blinds/curtains
- Thoughtful additions throughout creates a contemporary feel, ideal for first home
- Single lock-up garage set within the secure basement, established garden complex
- Double brick construction with solid walls, quiet and private top-floor position
- Private and pet-safe large balcony with steel-reinforced safety netting installed
- Easy walk to local schools, parklands, Mount Druitt Hospital, Timezone and cinemas

Strata rates: $1,195 per quarter
Water rates: $216 per quarter
Council rates: $489 per quarter

6 DRUITT STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
Sold By Umer Chaudhry & Shaikh Faisal!

Call or text on 0470470833 for an updated appraisal!


Welcome to 6 Druitt Street, Mount Druitt, three-bedroom home set on a 455 sqm block. This inviting property presents an exceptional opportunity for first-home buyers and astute investors looking to add a quality asset to their portfolio. Located in a sought-after area, this home offers comfort, convenience, and potential for future growth. Upon entering, you'll find a spacious lounge room, complete with split-system air conditioning, providing a welcoming environment. The kitchen, designed for functionality, boasts ample storage, a dishwasher, and a comfortable layout perfect for everyday living.


Highlight Features:
+ Spacious lounge plus additional dining area with split-system air conditioning
+ Comfortable kitchen with ample storage and a dishwasher
+ Three bedrooms ; two with split-system air conditioning & built in wardrobes
+ Functional central bathroom
+ Ideal for investors or first-home buyers
+ Great potential to personalise or add Granny flat (STCA)


Local Amenities
+ Mount Druitt Train Station 1.9km
+ Mount Druitt Westfield 1.9km
+ Mount Druitt Public School 1.3km
+ Mount Druitt Hospital 2.4km
+ Easy access to public transport and M7 & M4

Under Contract By Angelo @ Ray White United
2 DURHAM STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
Delivering exceptional versatility, comfort, and space, this impressive residence is perfectly designed for growing or extended families seeking flexible living options in a private and secure setting. Showcasing a spacious dual-level main home together with a separate self-contained retreat at the rear, the property offers the ideal balance of functionality and convenience with multiple living zones, generous outdoor space, and extensive off-street parking. Whether you are accommodating extended family, working from home, or simply looking for extra room to spread out, this unique property provides a lifestyle of comfort and practicality.

WHAT WE LOVE ABOUT THIS PROPERTY:

- Four well-sized bedrooms in the main residence, with two positioned downstairs and two upstairs
- Built-in wardrobes to three bedrooms for added storage and convenience
- Main downstairs bathroom with shower plus separate toilet for added practicality
- Second upstairs bathroom complemented by an attached walk-in robe
- Charming timber kitchen featuring dishwasher, electric cooking, ample cabinetry, and included fridge
- Ceiling fans installed throughout all bedrooms for year-round comfort
- Light-filled lounge room complete with air conditioning and ceiling fan
- Adjoining dining area ideal for everyday family meals and entertaining
- Additional upstairs living area perfect as a study, retreat, kids' zone, or second sitting room
- Plantation shutters fitted to most windows throughout the main house
- External laundry offering additional space and functionality
- Fully fenced yard providing privacy and security for the family
- Large garden shed available for tenant use
- Extensive off-street parking including front driveway parking for two vehicles
- Additional secure gated rear driveway accommodating up to three more vehicles
- Undercover rear carport ideal for extra vehicle protection
- Generous 543 sqm block size offering space, privacy, and flexibility

Retreat:
- Privately positioned one-bedroom partly furnished retreat at the rear
- Open-plan living and kitchenette area offering independent living potential
- Separate external shower and toilet facilities
- Comfortable bedroom featuring wardrobe and ceiling fan

- Currently rented for $700 per week, representing excellent immediate income potential, with market expectations indicating a return of approximately $850-$900 per week.

For more information on this property, please contact Angelo on 0425 361 696.

SOLD AT AUCTION BY DAVID FRENDO
12 GEORGE STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
David Frendo from R&W Elite Group proudly presents this exciting 1/4 acre property, set on a substantial 1,012m2 (approx.) level corner block with outstanding development potential and potential for a granny flat, subject to Council approval.

Offering a rare opportunity to secure approximately quarter acre in a highly convenient location, this three-bedroom clad home provides an ideal foundation for those looking to renovate, rebuild, add a granny flat or explore future development possibilities.

The existing residence features generous living spaces, original character and a practical floorplan, while the expansive level landholding, corner position and established gardens provide plenty of scope for those seeking a property with significant future potential.

Property features include:
* Substantial 1,012m2 (approx.) 1/4 acre block
* Level corner block with development potential
* Potential for granny flat, (S.T.C.A)
* Development potential, subject to Council approval
* Three generous bedrooms with carpet flooring
* Separate lounge with split-system air conditioning
* Dining area adjoining the original kitchen
* Original kitchen with electric cooking
* Enclosed screened sunroom providing additional living space
* High ceilings throughout
* Internal laundry with convenient access
* Single tandem garage with additional workspace
* Covered greenhouse and shade-cloth garden area
* Outdoor outhouse toilet
* Established mature gardens
* Excellent scope to renovate or redevelop
* Potential rental return of $700 per week

A rare quarter-acre opportunity with genuine potential, this property is ideally suited to developers, investors, renovators and families looking to secure a substantial parcel of land in a tightly held location.

With its 1,012m2 (approx.) level corner block, generous proportions and potential for a granny flat or future development, subject to Council approval, the property offers an exciting opportunity to unlock the full value of this impressive landholding.

Ideally located within close proximity to Mt Druitt Railway Station, local shops, schools, parks and public transport, with easy access to the M4 and M7 Motorways, this property combines exceptional land potential with everyday convenience.

Properties offering approximately one-quarter of an acre, corner positioning and granny flat potential are increasingly difficult to find.

AUCTION: Saturday - 12th September, On Site at 2:30pm

For further information, please contact David Frendo on 0411 457 027.

SOLD BY BASEL NAHAS & SINA TAFA
130 MOUNT DRUITT ROAD, MOUNT DRUITT
Occupying a gated corner block in a peaceful cul-de-sac setting, this blonde-brick residence offers a comfortable foundation with exciting scope to personalise and enhance. Original character is complemented by timber floors, highlight windows and a practical layout, plus the detached retreat with storage adds valuable flexibility. Set on 563sqm block with no easements, the property presents an appealing first-home or investment opportunity with potential for a granny flat (STCA).

- Open living flows naturally to the kitchen/dining zone, creating a practical layout
- Child-friendly yard offers plenty of space for outdoor enjoyment or enhancements
- Set on a 563sqm block with no easements, the generous corner block is fully gated
- Original galley-style kitchen with a cooktop connects directly with the dining space
- Three comfortable bedrooms enjoy timber floors, one fitted with a built-in wardrobe
- Air conditioning, fully tiled bathroom features a shower and fixed mirrored cabinetry
- Polished timber floors, highlight windows, air conditioning and an external laundry
- Detached garage conversion offers additional storage and a w/c, off-street parking
- Original blonde-brick construction, polished timber flooring and highlight windows
- Providing a blank canvas for a contemporary transformation in a sought-after area
- Substantial 563sqm corner block may also suit the addition of a granny flat (STCA)
- Positioned on a quiet cul-de-sac corner block close to the M4 and M7 motorways

With Westfield Shopping Centre located directly opposite the complex, it provides added convenience for those in search for a property within a short distance to shops, station and more!

Additional Features Include:
* Light-filled open plan lounge and dining room with air-conditioning and floating floors

Outgoings:
* Strata Levies: $810.00 Per Quarter

Brand New Luxury Residence - Sophisticated Family Living in the Heart of Cabramatta
21 LINKS AVENUE, CABRAMATTA
21 Links Avenue, Cabramatta NSW

A breathtaking showcase of contemporary luxury, exceptional craftsmanship, and thoughtful design, this brand-new residence at 21 Links Avenue, Cabramatta presents an outstanding opportunity for families seeking space, comfort, and prestige in a highly sought-after location.

Set on approximately 316sqm; of land with an impressive of internal living space, this beautifully appointed home has been meticulously designed to deliver modern family living at its finest. Featuring five generous bedrooms, four luxurious bathrooms, and multiple living zones, the residence effortlessly combines elegance, functionality, and comfort.

From the moment you arrive, the striking facade and quality construction make a lasting impression. Inside, soaring high ceilings, premium finishes, and an abundance of natural light create a sophisticated and welcoming atmosphere throughout.

At the heart of the home lies a stunning gourmet kitchen featuring premium Santorini stone benchtops, quality appliances, a massive walk-in pantry, and an adjoining outdoor BBQ area, making it ideal for both everyday family life and entertaining on a grand scale.

Step outside to discover a substantial backyard rarely found in newly built homes. Offering ample space for family gatherings, celebrations, and outdoor entertaining, the expansive yard also provides plenty of room for children and pets to enjoy.

Property Features
• Brand-new luxury residence with premium finishes throughout
• Five spacious bedrooms with built-in wardrobes
• Four designer bathrooms, including three private ensuites
• Three bedrooms with their own bathroom access
• Multiple living and dining areas designed for growing families
• Gourmet kitchen with premium Santorini stone benchtops and quality appliances

• Elegant curved architectural features throughout the home
• Skylights in showers, providing abundant natural light
• Skylights in walk-in wardrobes

• Ample parking accommodation for multiple vehicles
• Expansive backyard ideal for entertaining and family enjoyment
• Approx. 316sqm land size




Prime Location

Conveniently positioned close to local schools, shopping centres, parks, cafes, and public transport, this exceptional residence offers the perfect balance of luxury, lifestyle, and convenience in one of Cabramatta's most desirable locations.

A rare opportunity to secure a brand-new family home of exceptional quality and scale. Inspect today and experience luxury living at its finest.

SOLD BY MESHEL BAHNAM & ANISHA FERNANDO ! 0478 664 123
44/78 METHVEN STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
Anisha Fernando & Meshel Bahnam of the Ray White United Group are proud to present to the market 44/78 Methven Street, Mount Druitt!

Whether you're stepping into the market, looking to downsize, or searching for a smart investment, 44/78 Methven Street, Mount Druitt is a home that offers comfort, practicality, and a location that makes everyday life easy.

Set across two levels, the home provides a well-balanced layout with the living and dining areas downstairs, creating a welcoming space to gather with family or unwind after a busy day. The kitchen is both practical and functional, with plenty of storage and easy access to the outdoor patio, making indoor-outdoor living effortless.

Upstairs, three generously sized bedrooms, all fitted with built-in robes, offer peaceful retreats, while the main bathroom is complemented by the convenience of two separate WCs-one on the ground floor and one upstairs-making busy mornings that little bit easier.

Outside, you'll find a private patio that's perfect for entertaining or enjoying a quiet afternoon, along with a secure garage complete with additional storage, an internal laundry, a handy backyard shed, and a welcoming front porch that adds to the home's appeal.

Located within easy reach of Westfield Mount Druitt, Mount Druitt Station, quality schools, parks, and Mount Druitt Hospital, you'll enjoy the convenience of having everything you need just moments from your doorstep.

SOLD BY UMER CHAUDHRY 0470470833!
4/7 BAYNES STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
SOLD BY UMER CHAUDHRY 0470470833!

CALL/TEXT ME ON 0470470833 FOR AN UPDATED APPRAISAL



Welcome to 4/7 Bayne Street, Mount Druitt - a freestanding three-bedroom, two-bathroom residence offering comfort, space, and convenience.
Freshly painted throughout, the home presents a bright and updated feel from the moment you walk in. The generous living area connects to the master bedroom, which includes an ensuite, built-in wardrobe, ducted air conditioning, and access to a central vacuum system. Spacious dining and family zones provide plenty of room for everyday living, with air conditioning servicing both areas.
The kitchen is well-appointed with gas cooking, an oven and dishwasher. The two remaining bedrooms both feature built-in wardrobes and floorboards. Outdoors, the large, covered pergola and concreted backyard offering low - maintainance which also creates an ideal setting for entertaining, relaxing, or enjoying extra outdoor space.

With no strata fees and a freestanding design, this property offers a rare opportunity in a convenient Mount Druitt location.

Key Features:
• Freestanding home with no strata fees
• Spacious open-plan, combined living and dining areas
• Master bedroom with ensuite and built-in wardrobe
• Two additional bedrooms with built-in wardrobes and floorboards
• Kitchen with gas cooking, oven, and dishwasher
• Ducted air conditioning throughout
• Freshly painted interior
• Large, covered backyard pergola plus yard space plus low maintenance
• Community levy approximately $155 per quarter
• 11.6KW Solar Panels to reduce your electricity bills
• Double garage plus double carport
• Five visitor parking spaces

Nearby Amenities:
• Mount Druitt Train Station – 1.8km

• Mount Druitt Hospital – 3.8km

First floor unit in a prime location
6/34 HILL STREET, CABRAMATTA
This beautiful unit offers an exceptional blend of style, comfort, and convenience. Located in the heart of Cabramatta, it provides easy access to essential amenities, making it the perfect home for professionals, couples, or small families.

Contemporary Sub-Penthouse With Sweeping Ocean Views
12/19 YOUNG STREET, VAUCLUSE
This superb sub-penthouse-style apartment combines streamlined contemporary design and high-end finishes with magnificent ocean views, in a sought-after Vaucluse enclave. Overlooking Diamond Bay Reserve towards the coastline, with sweeping district views, the stunning 2-bedroom home occupies a leafy corner position, offering both sanctuary and exceptional entertaining potential.

Set across 2 levels of boutique "The Carlton", this excellent security apartment comes complete with underground parking and easy lift access. The upper level is bathed in natural light, with skylights, timber floors and glass windows all enhancing the expansive proportions. A large chef's kitchen, appointed with Smeg gas appliances and concealed laundry, features a custom dining table built into the marble island bench. The living space flows through floor-to-ceiling glass doors onto a vast 30sqm (approx.) terrace, creating a seamless entertaining experience, perfect for alfresco summers spent enjoying the magnificent views.

Two double bedrooms are peacefully positioned on the lower level, both with luxe travertine ensuites and built-in robes. One enjoys a private leafy terrace and bathroom with bespoke Corian vanity, whilst the 2nd includes a full-sized bath. Close to Vaucluse village shops and cafes and the majestic Diamond Bay cliffs, central to Bondi, Watson's Bay, and harbour beaches, this home presents a sophisticated retreat, perfectly positioned for exceptional coastal living.

- 2 bed | 2.5 bath | 1 car | Lift access
- Stunning 2-bed sub-penthouse apartment in The Carlton
- Sweeping ocean views across Diamond Bay Reserve
- Expansive proportions, lovely light-filled living space
- 30sqm (approx.) entertainer's deck, inside/outside flow
- Chef's kitchen feat. Smeg appliances, concealed laundry
- Marble benchtops plus chic custom-built dining table
- Both bedrooms with ensuites, guest powder room
- One bedroom enjoys access to a private leafy terrace
- Security building, undercover parking, lift access, A/C
- Pet-friendly, close to Vaucluse village, coastal walks
- Central to Bondi, Watson's Bay, harbourside, beaches
- Tranquil haven with exceptional entertaining scope

SOLD BY MESHEL BAHNAM ! 0478 664 123
4 EDITH STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
Meshel Bahnam of the Ray White United Group is proud to present to the market 4 Edith St, Mount Druitt!

Set in a convenient pocket of Mount Druitt, 4 Edith Street offers a fantastic opportunity for families seeking space, flexibility, and excellent outdoor entertaining, along with the added benefit of a fully enclosed tennis court. With multiple living zones and a practical layout designed to accommodate a growing family, this home combines comfort and functionality in a location close to everything you need.

Inside, you'll find four well-sized bedrooms with built-in robes, a spacious living area, dedicated dining space, and the unique advantage of having kitchens on both the ground and upper levels. Whether you're accommodating extended family, entertaining guests, or simply enjoying the added convenience, this versatile setup provides endless possibilities. A generous rumpus room and sunroom further enhance the home's appeal, offering additional space to relax, work, or entertain.

The outdoor areas are equally impressive, featuring a pergola, patio, and BBQ area that create the perfect setting for family gatherings, weekend barbecues, and year-round entertaining. Completing the package are a secure garage, internal laundry, and storage shed, ensuring there is plenty of room for everyday practicality.

Positioned within walking distance of schools, parks, shops, and public transport, this is a home that delivers both lifestyle and convenience for families looking to establish themselves in one of Mount Druitt's most accessible locations.

WHAT WE LOVE ABOUT THIS PROPERTY:
*Four bedrooms with built-in robes

SOLD BY LOCAL AGENT ANKIT BOHORA 0450507974
52 PAULL STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
Award winning local agent Ankit Bohora from Laing + Simmons The Abassi Group is excited to present 52 Paull Street, Mount Druitt, a rare north facing opportunity set on an expansive 1,012sqm parcel with endless future potential. Offering a well kept family home with multiple living spaces and an enormous backyard, this is the kind of property that opens the door to renovate, extend, rebuild or explore exciting development opportunities subject to council approval.

From a development perspective, the property offers the potential to construct a duplex, subdivide, or build two separate dwellings (STCA). With its wide 20m frontage and substantial land size, there is also scope to build two sets of duplexes subject to council approval, making this an exceptional opportunity for developers looking to maximise the site's potential. Alternatively, the block also lends itself perfectly to the construction of a large family home with the option of adding a granny flat, ideal for growing or multi-generational families.

Positioned in a convenient pocket of Mount Druitt, the home is moments from local schools, shopping centres, public transport and major arterial roads while still offering a quiet family friendly setting. Large north facing blocks like this are becoming harder to find, making this an exciting opportunity for families, investors and developers looking to secure a substantial piece of land in a high growth location.

Features Include:
+ 20m Frontage approx.
+ Three well sized bedrooms, all fitted with built in wardrobes and ceiling fans
+ Functional kitchen featuring gas cooking, generous bench space and plenty of storage
+ Spacious main living area complete with reverse cycle air conditioning and ceiling fan
+ Separate dining area filled with natural light
+ Large sunroom offering additional living or entertaining space
+ Neat and tidy bathroom with shower, vanity and toilet
+ Internal laundry with second toilet
+ Double tandem drive through garage with plenty of off street parking
+ Expansive backyard with endless potential for families, entertaining or future possibilities subject to council approval
+ Current rental potential of $550 - $570 per week approx.

For further information please contact our friendly staff on 9673 2200.

- Living and Dining Areas: Three expansive living spaces that promote a harmonious flow, ideal for gatherings and relaxation.

- Outdoor Spaces: Oversized outdoor entertaining area with a sparkling in-ground pool, large backyard, and citrus trees, perfect for outdoor enjoyment.

- Additional Features: Brick construction two-bedroom granny flat, garage converted into a self-contained studio or potential third residence, and large side access to fit up to 10 vehicles. Steel frame built throughout the house. Solar panels installed for energy efficiency.

- Location: Minutes to the Great Western Highway, Mount Druitt train station, and the upcoming Badgerys Creek Airport, offering a convenient lifestyle.

Stylish & Convenient Townhouse in a Prime Cabramatta Location
21/96-100 LONGFIELD STREET, CABRAMATTA
Perfectly positioned in a well-maintained complex, this inviting two-bedroom townhouse presents an outstanding opportunity for first home buyers, savvy investors, or those seeking a low-maintenance lifestyle. Offering comfort, convenience, and excellent value this home is ideally located within easy reach of local shops, schools, public transport, parks, and the vibrant Cabramatta CBD.

The thoughtfully designed floorplan features two generously sized bedrooms, a neat and functional bathroom a spacious open-plan living and dining area, and a well-appointed kitchen with ample storage. Outside enjoy a private courtyard perfect for relaxing or entertaining while the secure lock-up garage provides added convenience and peace of mind.

Whether you're looking to move straight in or add a quality property to your investment portfolio this townhouse offers the perfect blend of lifestyle and location.

SOLD BY MESHEL BAHNAM ! 0478 664 123
20A DERWENT STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
Meshel Bahnam of the Ray White United Group is proud to present to the market 20A Derwent Street, Mount Druitt !

If you've been searching for a home that offers room for the whole family without compromising on convenience, 20A Derwent Street, Mount Druitt is one you won't want to miss. Beautifully presented and thoughtfully designed, this spacious residence delivers the flexibility and functionality that modern families are looking for.

From the moment you step inside, you'll appreciate the generous proportions and light-filled interiors. The open-plan living and dining areas create a welcoming atmosphere, while the kitchen is perfectly positioned to keep you connected with family and guests. With four well-sized bedrooms, each featuring built-in robes, there's plenty of space for everyone, while the master bedroom enjoys the added privacy of its own ensuite.

Outside, the patio offers a great place to enjoy your morning coffee, host weekend barbecues, or simply unwind after a long day. Automatic remote-controlled garage, handy shed, internal laundry, and low-maintenance yard complete a home that's designed for easy everyday living.

Whether you're upsizing, accommodating extended family, or looking for a quality investment, this home offers the space, comfort, and location to suit a wide range of buyers. Move in and enjoy everything this fantastic Mount Druitt address has to offer.

Brand New - A Statement of Luxury, Space & Sophistication
3 GRACE AVENUE, CABRAMATTA
A breathtaking fusion of visionary design, uncompromising craftsmanship and refined luxury, this brand-new architectural masterpiece delivers an unrivalled family lifestyle. Masterfully crafted over two spectacular levels, the residence showcases grand proportions, soaring light-filled interiors and an effortless flow between multiple living and dining spaces, seamlessly extending to resort-inspired outdoor entertaining.

Positioned on a generous elevated 556sqm parcel, this exceptional residence enjoys a peaceful yet highly convenient setting, just moments from Cabramatta High School, Cabramatta CBD, Train Station, and a comprehensive range of local amenities.

Every detail has been meticulously considered to create a home of timeless sophistication, where premium finishes, premium inclusions, and exceptional functionality combine to deliver one of the area's finest luxury residences.

Property Highlights

-Brand-new architectural masterpiece showcasing exceptional luxury, scale and craftsmanship.
-Expansive open-plan living and dining areas enhanced by extensive glazing, flooding the home with natural light.
-Striking interiors featuring a double-height statement wall with LED inlays, and exquisite designer chandeliers.
-High ceilings throughout, complemented by a spectacular double-height void adjoining the upstairs family retreat.
-Showpiece gourmet kitchen appointed with premium appliances, striking stone benchtops and splashback, integrated dishwasher, generous butler's pantry and abundant custom cabinetry.
-Palatial master retreat complete with a private balcony, walk-in wardrobe and luxurious designer ensuite.
-In addition to the luxurious master retreat, the upper level features three further oversized bedrooms, including a second bedroom with its own private ensuite, while a guest bedroom downstairs also enjoys a private ensuite.
-Designer bathrooms on both levels finished with premium fixtures and elegant appointments.
-Expansive covered alfresco entertaining area featuring a fully equipped outdoor kitchen, dedicated seating area, and a generous grassed backyard, creating the perfect setting for year-round family living and entertaining.
-Remote double lock-up garage with additional driveway parking and automatic security gate.
-Keyless entry, smart home automation and automatic sheer curtains.
-Ducted zoned air conditioning, alarm system and intercom for year-round comfort and security.

SOLD BY LOCAL AGENT ANKIT BOHORA 0450507974
52 PAULL STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
Award winning local agent Ankit Bohora from Laing + Simmons The Abassi Group is excited to present 52 Paull Street, Mount Druitt, a rare north facing opportunity set on an expansive 1,012sqm parcel with endless future potential. Offering a well kept family home with multiple living spaces and an enormous backyard, this is the kind of property that opens the door to renovate, extend, rebuild or explore exciting development opportunities subject to council approval.

From a development perspective, the property offers the potential to construct a duplex, subdivide, or build two separate dwellings (STCA). With its wide 20m frontage and substantial land size, there is also scope to build two sets of duplexes subject to council approval, making this an exceptional opportunity for developers looking to maximise the site's potential. Alternatively, the block also lends itself perfectly to the construction of a large family home with the option of adding a granny flat, ideal for growing or multi-generational families.

Positioned in a convenient pocket of Mount Druitt, the home is moments from local schools, shopping centres, public transport and major arterial roads while still offering a quiet family friendly setting. Large north facing blocks like this are becoming harder to find, making this an exciting opportunity for families, investors and developers looking to secure a substantial piece of land in a high growth location.

Features Include:
+ 20m Frontage approx.
+ Three well sized bedrooms, all fitted with built in wardrobes and ceiling fans
+ Functional kitchen featuring gas cooking, generous bench space and plenty of storage
+ Spacious main living area complete with reverse cycle air conditioning and ceiling fan
+ Separate dining area filled with natural light
+ Large sunroom offering additional living or entertaining space
+ Neat and tidy bathroom with shower, vanity and toilet
+ Internal laundry with second toilet
+ Double tandem drive through garage with plenty of off street parking
+ Expansive backyard with endless potential for families, entertaining or future possibilities subject to council approval
+ Current rental potential of $550 - $570 per week approx.

For further information please contact our friendly staff on 9673 2200.

SOLD BY MESHEL BAHNAM ! 0478 664 123
4 EDITH STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
Meshel Bahnam of the Ray White United Group is proud to present to the market 4 Edith St, Mount Druitt!

Set in a convenient pocket of Mount Druitt, 4 Edith Street offers a fantastic opportunity for families seeking space, flexibility, and excellent outdoor entertaining, along with the added benefit of a fully enclosed tennis court. With multiple living zones and a practical layout designed to accommodate a growing family, this home combines comfort and functionality in a location close to everything you need.

Inside, you'll find four well-sized bedrooms with built-in robes, a spacious living area, dedicated dining space, and the unique advantage of having kitchens on both the ground and upper levels. Whether you're accommodating extended family, entertaining guests, or simply enjoying the added convenience, this versatile setup provides endless possibilities. A generous rumpus room and sunroom further enhance the home's appeal, offering additional space to relax, work, or entertain.

The outdoor areas are equally impressive, featuring a pergola, patio, and BBQ area that create the perfect setting for family gatherings, weekend barbecues, and year-round entertaining. Completing the package are a secure garage, internal laundry, and storage shed, ensuring there is plenty of room for everyday practicality.

Positioned within walking distance of schools, parks, shops, and public transport, this is a home that delivers both lifestyle and convenience for families looking to establish themselves in one of Mount Druitt's most accessible locations.

WHAT WE LOVE ABOUT THIS PROPERTY:
*Four bedrooms with built-in robes
*Two Well-appointed bathroom
*Secure garage
*Spacious living area
*Separate dining area
*Kitchens on both the ground and upper levels
*Large rumpus room
*Bright sunroom
*Covered pergola
*Outdoor patio
*BBQ area
*Full size Tennis Court (hard surface court on concrete), fully enclosed high fences, entry gates at both ends, with proper drainage and surrounding landscape.
*Internal laundry
*Storage shed

SOME LOCAL AMENITIES:
*Sacred Heart Primary School - Approx. 250m
*Colyton Public School - Approx. 550m
*Rupertswood Park - Approx. 600m
*Mount Druitt Station - Approx. 1.0km

*Westfield Mount Druitt - Approx. 1.2km
*Mount Druitt Public School - Approx. 1.5km
*Mount Druitt Hospital - Approx. 2.4km
*Colyton High School - Approx. 2.5km
*RAAF Memorial Park - Approx. 2.7km
*Rooty Hill High School - Approx. 2.8km
*Oxley Park Public School - Approx. 3.1km
*Whalan Public School - Approx. 3.1km
*Rooty Hill Station - Approx. 3.2km
*St Agnes Catholic High School - Approx. 3.9km
*Poplar Park - Approx. 4.4km
*St Marys Station - Approx. 4.6km
*St Marys Village Shopping Centre - Approx. 4.9km

Contemporary Sub-Penthouse With Sweeping Ocean Views
12/19 YOUNG STREET, VAUCLUSE
This superb sub-penthouse-style apartment combines streamlined contemporary design and high-end finishes with magnificent ocean views, in a sought-after Vaucluse enclave. Overlooking Diamond Bay Reserve towards the coastline, with sweeping district views, the stunning 2-bedroom home occupies a leafy corner position, offering both sanctuary and exceptional entertaining potential.

Set across 2 levels of boutique "The Carlton", this excellent security apartment comes complete with underground parking and easy lift access. The upper level is bathed in natural light, with skylights, timber floors and glass windows all enhancing the expansive proportions. A large chef's kitchen, appointed with Smeg gas appliances and concealed laundry, features a custom dining table built into the marble island bench. The living space flows through floor-to-ceiling glass doors onto a vast 30sqm (approx.) terrace, creating a seamless entertaining experience, perfect for alfresco summers spent enjoying the magnificent views.

Two double bedrooms are peacefully positioned on the lower level, both with luxe travertine ensuites and built-in robes. One enjoys a private leafy terrace and bathroom with bespoke Corian vanity, whilst the 2nd includes a full-sized bath. Close to Vaucluse village shops and cafes and the majestic Diamond Bay cliffs, central to Bondi, Watson's Bay, and harbour beaches, this home presents a sophisticated retreat, perfectly positioned for exceptional coastal living.

- 2 bed | 2.5 bath | 1 car | Lift access
- Stunning 2-bed sub-penthouse apartment in The Carlton
- Sweeping ocean views across Diamond Bay Reserve
- Expansive proportions, lovely light-filled living space
- 30sqm (approx.) entertainer's deck, inside/outside flow
- Chef's kitchen feat. Smeg appliances, concealed laundry
- Marble benchtops plus chic custom-built dining table
- Both bedrooms with ensuites, guest powder room
- One bedroom enjoys access to a private leafy terrace
- Security building, undercover parking, lift access, A/C
- Pet-friendly, close to Vaucluse village, coastal walks
- Central to Bondi, Watson's Bay, harbourside, beaches
- Tranquil haven with exceptional entertaining scope

Property Description
Another Block Sold by the Apartment Block Specialists
An incredible opportunity to acquire the entire building, this boutique full brick character block delivers four spacious apartments offering immense scope for duplexes, a brand new luxury residence, refurbishment or for multi-generational family lifestyles (STCA). The current layout comprises charming apartments with semi-like proportions, original period features and access to the communal garden. Set over a 696.77 sqm block with a wide 15.24 metre frontage, it's an easy walk to Vaucluse village shops, cafes, eateries and city transport.

• 9-11 bedrooms, 5 bathrooms
• Set in heart of Vaucluse, quiet tree lined street
• 3x 2 bedroom apartments, 1 x 3 bedrooms
• All with spacious living and sunrooms
• Three bedroom apartment with stylish updates
• Gas kitchens, generous bedrooms, homely ambiance
• Classic appeal, high ceilings, ocean glimpses
• Currently returning $201,084pa with potential to increase
• Close to leading schools and coastal parklands

Property Description
A rare harbourside sanctuary on Parsley Bay
Offered for the first time in almost 50 years, this much-loved family home presents an extremely rare opportunity to secure a truly unique harbourside lifestyle in one of Vaucluse's most sought-after pockets. Privately secluded with direct access into Parsley Bay Reserve, it offers a wonderful connection to nature while remaining conveniently close to a range of schools, amenities and transport options.

- A back gate opening onto parkland that leads down to the park & beach
- Embraced by nature with lovely leafy outlooks in all directions
- A versatile two-storey layout and living spaces on both levels
- Light-filled interiors feature excellent crossflow ventilation
- Spacious entertainment areas and ultra-private grassed garden
- 5 bedrooms, 3 bathrooms plus a well-equipped kitchen
- A separate lower-level retreat is ideal for teenagers or guests
- Immaculately presented with plenty of scope to make your own
- Secluded back from the road in a private and quiet environment
- Walk to harbour foreshore, historic laneways and walking tracks

Land Size 646m2 approx.

Council Rates: $752pq
Water Rates: $202pq

Property Description
Contemporary Sub-Penthouse With Sweeping Ocean Views
This superb sub-penthouse-style apartment combines streamlined contemporary design and high-end finishes with magnificent ocean views, in a sought-after Vaucluse enclave. Overlooking Diamond Bay Reserve towards the coastline, with sweeping district views, the stunning 2-bedroom home occupies a leafy corner position, offering both sanctuary and exceptional entertaining potential.

Set across 2 levels of boutique "The Carlton", this excellent security apartment comes complete with underground parking and easy lift access. The upper level is bathed in natural light, with skylights, timber floors and glass windows all enhancing the expansive proportions. A large chef's kitchen, appointed with Smeg gas appliances and concealed laundry, features a custom dining table built into the marble island bench. The living space flows through floor-to-ceiling glass doors onto a vast 30sqm (approx.) terrace, creating a seamless entertaining experience, perfect for alfresco summers spent enjoying the magnificent views.

Two double bedrooms are peacefully positioned on the lower level, both with luxe travertine ensuites and built-in robes. One enjoys a private leafy terrace and bathroom with bespoke Corian vanity, whilst the 2nd includes a full-sized bath. Close to Vaucluse village shops and cafes and the majestic Diamond Bay cliffs, central to Bondi, Watson's Bay, and harbour beaches, this home presents a sophisticated retreat, perfectly positioned for exceptional coastal living.

- 2 bed | 2.5 bath | 1 car | Lift access
- Stunning 2-bed sub-penthouse apartment in The Carlton
- Sweeping ocean views across Diamond Bay Reserve
- Expansive proportions, lovely light-filled living space
- 30sqm (approx.) entertainer's deck, inside/outside flow
- Chef's kitchen feat. Smeg appliances, concealed laundry
- Marble benchtops plus chic custom-built dining table
- Both bedrooms with ensuites, guest powder room
- One bedroom enjoys access to a private leafy terrace
- Security building, undercover parking, lift access, A/C
- Pet-friendly, close to Vaucluse village, coastal walks
- Central to Bondi, Watson's Bay, harbourside, beaches
- Tranquil haven with exceptional entertaining scope

Property Description
Stylish & Convenient Townhouse in a Prime Cabramatta Location
Perfectly positioned in a well-maintained complex, this inviting two-bedroom townhouse presents an outstanding opportunity for first home buyers, savvy investors, or those seeking a low-maintenance lifestyle. Offering comfort, convenience, and excellent value this home is ideally located within easy reach of local shops, schools, public transport, parks, and the vibrant Cabramatta CBD.

The thoughtfully designed floorplan features two generously sized bedrooms, a neat and functional bathroom a spacious open-plan living and dining area, and a well-appointed kitchen with ample storage. Outside enjoy a private courtyard perfect for relaxing or entertaining while the secure lock-up garage provides added convenience and peace of mind.

Whether you're looking to move straight in or add a quality property to your investment portfolio this townhouse offers the perfect blend of lifestyle and location.

Property Features:
- Spacious open-plan living and dining area designed for comfortable everyday living
- Functional kitchen featuring ample bench space generous cupboard storage and quality appliances
- Two generously sized bedrooms both with plenty of natural light
- Well-appointed bathroom with floor to ceiling tiles
- Additional outside powder room
- Internal laundry with plenty of space
- Split-system air conditioning for year-round comfort
- Tiled flooring downstairs with carpeted bedrooms for added warmth and comfort
- Abundant natural light throughout with a practical and family-friendly floorplan
- Private low-maintenance courtyard ideal for entertaining, outdoor dining or relaxing
- Secure lock-up garage

Property Description
Convenient, Low-Maintenance Living in a Prime Cabramatta Location
Professionals Cabramatta proudly presents this well-positioned Level 1 unit, offering a practical layout, low-maintenance lifestyle, and outstanding convenience just moments from the heart of Cabramatta.
Set within a quiet complex of only 8 units, this property is ideal for first home buyers, downsizers, or investors seeking a solid opportunity in a high-demand location. With individual title ownership, it provides both security and long-term value.
The home features two well-proportioned bedrooms, including a master with walk-in robe and air conditioning for added comfort. The living and dining area is designed for easy everyday living, complemented by a functional kitchen and natural light throughout.
Positioned on the first floor, the unit offers a sense of privacy while maintaining accessibility. A single carport adds convenience, while the low-maintenance nature of the property makes it perfect for those with a busy lifestyle or those looking for a straightforward investment.
Located just 600 metres to Cabramatta Station, and within close proximity to local shops, schools, and amenities, this property delivers a lifestyle of ease and connectivity.

Key Features:
2 bedrooms | 1 bathroom | 1 carport
Master with walk-in robe & air conditioning
Level 1 position in a small complex of 8 units
Individual title ownership
Low-maintenance living
Approx. 600m to Cabramatta Station
Close to primary schools & Canley Vale High School
Strata Fee: Approximate $400/quarter

An excellent opportunity to secure a well-located property with strong rental and growth potential.

DUAL INCOME PROPERTY | PERFECT LOCATION | STRONG RENTAL YIELD
Jayden Kiet and the team at Ray White United Group proudly present this exceptional dual-occupancy opportunity at 2 & 2A Ophir Grove, Mount Druitt. Positioned on a generous parcel of land, this property offers a spacious three-bedroom home alongside a well-designed two-bedroom granny flat - perfect for investors seeking strong returns or families looking for flexible living arrangements.

Thoughtfully designed for comfort and practicality, both dwellings provide functional layouts, private outdoor spaces, and excellent separation - making this a smart, income-generating asset or multi-generational living solution.

Main Residence Features:
- Three well-proportioned bedrooms, all with built-in wardrobes
- Bright and spacious living area, ideal for everyday living
- Separate dining space flowing from the kitchen
- Additional family/rumpus room offering flexibility for entertaining or relaxation
- Neat and modern kitchen with ample storage
- Central bathroom plus laundry with additional toilet
- Covered porch and large outdoor patio area
- Split system air conditioning for year-round comfort
- Single lock-up garage with driveway access
- Detached shed providing extra storage
- Solar Panels for energy savings

Granny Flat Features:
- Two comfortable well-sized bedrooms, both with built-in wardrobes
- Open-plan living and dining area filled with natural light
- Combined bathroom and laundry for convenience
- Split system air conditioning for year-round comfort
- Privately positioned with its own access

Investment Snapshot:
- Main house currently returning approx. $600 per week
- Granny flat currently returning approx. $480 per week
- Dual income potential with separate dwellings, totalling over $1080 weekly return

Location Highlights:
- Approx. 1.6km to Mount Druitt Train Station, offering direct access to Sydney CBD via the Western Line
- Close to Mount Druitt Town Centre & Mount Druitt Central
- Easy access to multiple bus routes connecting surrounding suburbs and key amenities
- Aproxx. 2.9km to Mount Druitt Hospital
- Short drive to Westfield Mount Druitt
- Surrounded by local schools including Colyton Public School and Chifley College campuses
- Convenient access to the M4 Motorway for seamless commuting across Western Sydney
- Close proximity to parks, reserves, and family-friendly recreational spaces

Offering versatility, space, and strong rental appeal, this is a rare opportunity to secure a dual-income property in a convenient and growing location.

Contact Jayden Kiet on 0413 168 999 to arrange your inspection today.

A Landmark Luxury Residence of Palatial Scale in the Heart of Cabramatta
81 CUMBERLAND STREET, CABRAMATTA
Commanding attention from the moment you arrive, this grand luxury residence delivers a rare combination of palatial proportions, uncompromising craftsmanship and enduring full double brick and concrete construction — all set on an expansive parcel of over 1,200m².

Designed for buyers who demand space, prestige and permanence, the home showcases an impressive sense of scale throughout, with grand living domains, refined architectural detail and a level of construction rarely found in modern residences. Every element speaks to quality, from its solid foundations to its carefully considered finishes, creating a home built not only for today’s luxury lifestyle but for generations to come.

Positioned only a short distance from Cabramatta CBD and train station, this exceptional property offers the perfect balance of privacy, convenience and status. Enjoy effortless access to shopping, dining, transport and essential amenities while retreating to a residence that feels worlds apart in size, presence and distinction.

Set on substantial land, the property also presents outstanding potential for those seeking a prestigious family estate, multi-generational living or a blue-chip holding in one of the area’s most connected locations.

Property highlights:

- Grand luxury home with commanding street presence
- Palatial proportions and expansive living spaces
- Unrivalled craftsmanship and enduring quality
- Full double brick and concrete construction
- Set on over 1,200m² of premium land
- Moments to Cabramatta CBD, train station, shops and dining
- Ideal for luxury buyers, large families and prestige investors

A residence of this calibre is seldom offered — grand in scale, solid in construction and superbly positioned for lifestyle and legacy.

A rare opportunity to secure a substantial family home with exceptional lifestyle appeal & significant future potential in a coveted Vaucluse address
21 TOWNS ROAD, VAUCLUSE
Grand Family Estate occupying an impressive 1,132sqm parcel of beautifully established, park-like grounds, this distinguished five-bedroom family residence presents a rare combination of character, scale and privacy in one of Vaucluse's most peaceful settings.

Offering a substantial two-storey layout with immediate comfort and exceptional future potential, the home is just moments from village shops, harbour foreshore walks and the dramatic coastal cliffside trails.

• Expansive level landholding of 1,132sqm with dual occupancy potential (STCA)
• Comfortable as is, with outstanding scope to renovate, recreate or capitalise
• Elegant formal lounge and dining rooms flow to generous family living spaces
• Light-filled interiors enjoy a prized north-to-rear aspect and garden outlooks
• Stone-appointed kitchen featuring a Rosieres Paul Bocuse gas cooker
• Sun-washed alfresco entertaining area overlooking vast landscaped gardens
• Generously proportioned bedrooms, most with built-in or walk-in wardrobes
• Dedicated home office/study and multiple bathrooms, including a renovated upper-level bathroom
• Air conditioning, underfloor heating and soaring ornate ceilings throughout
• Tandem lock-up garage with drive-through access and secure gated entry

Outgoings:
Council Rates: $1,169.45 p/q approx.
Water Rates: $201.90 p/q approx.

Inspection: Saturday & Wednesday 12:00pm-12:45pm

site Auction: Saturday, 4th July 2026 at 12:00pm

Violet Farebrother 0407 66 99 70

Spacious, Fully Renovated & Low Strata – Walk to Station & CBD
10/118 CABRAMATTA ROAD, CABRAMATTA
Positioned in one of Cabramatta’s most convenient and high-demand pockets, this well-maintained top-floor apartment delivers an exceptional opportunity for first home buyers and investors alike.

Offering a practical layout and low-maintenance lifestyle, the home features two well-sized bedrooms, a bright open-plan living and dining area, and a functional kitchen. The property is neat, comfortable, and ready to move straight in or lease out immediately.

Set on the top floor of a solid and established complex, it enjoys added privacy with no neighbours above, enhancing both comfort and appeal.

🏡 Key Features:

- Total size 101m2 on title
- Two good-sized bedrooms
- Bright open-plan living and dining area
- Modern gourmet kitchen with lots of bench & storage space
- Renovated bathroom
- Internal laundry with 2nd toilet
- Extra-large 1.5 size lock-up garage
- Low strata $302 per quarter

📍 Location Highlights:

- Short walk to Cabramatta Train Station & shops
- Close to local schools and parks

💡 Why You’ll Love It:

A low-maintenance property in a blue-chip convenience location, this apartment is ideal for strong rental demand and long-term capital growth. Opportunities in this position are tightly held and rarely last long.

Uninterrupted Ocean Panorama From Top Floor Haven, Level Lift Access
6B/5-7 GIRILANG AVENUE, VAUCLUSE
With breathtaking ocean views from every room and a serene coastal ambience, this sun-washed apartment offers a rare chance in one of Vaucluse's most sought-after enclaves. Occupying a prized top-floor position in "Girilang", generous open-plan living and dining flows to a balcony capturing an uninterrupted 180-degree vista spanning Manly and North Head, across Diamond Bay and down to Bondi. Light-filled interiors are complemented by excellent proportions, with two bedrooms both featuring built-in robes, a spacious bathroom with concealed laundry, and a separate study providing ideal work-from-home flexibility.

The elevated setting and wrap of windows ensure constant natural light, sea breezes, and a sense of calm throughout. Set amid well-kept landscaped grounds, Girilang is an easy stroll to Vaucluse village cafés and shops, with pristine harbour parklands, coastal walks and beaches just minutes away. Complete with level lift access and allocated parking, and ready to enjoy immediately whilst also offering scope to update to taste (STCA), this is a superb opportunity to secure a tranquil haven in a blue-chip harbourside location.

- Sun-drenched top floor 2-bed w/ parking
- Uninterrupted 180-degree ocean views
- Level lift access, well-kept security building
- Open plan living and dining, timber floors
- Alfresco balcony enjoys ocean panorama
- Generous electric kitchen with dishwasher
- Stone counters and breakfast bar seating
- 2 light-filled bedrooms with built-in robes
- Spacious bathroom w/ shower over bath
- Study offers easy flexibility, internal laundry
- Well-maintained building, lovely grounds
- Stroll to Vaucluse village shops and cafes
- Mins to harbour parklands and beaches
- Enjoy as-is, with scope to update (STCA)

Ocean-view apartment / hotel-like comfort / secure parking
57/33 KIMBERLEY STREET, VAUCLUSE
Full-width windows deliver showstopping ocean and cliff views and abundant light throughout this apartment, a home of hotel-like comfort with secure parking on title. Perched high above Diamond Bay Reserve, the uninterrupted vistas create a remarkable sense of privacy, matched by the easy-care floorplan and breezy white interiors. At your door are stairs leading directly to the Diamond Bay Cliff Walk, or stroll to nearby village shops, cafés, beaches, elite schools and transport. A prestigious peninsula address in easy reach of exclusive Eastern Suburbs pockets, delivering a unique lifestyle along with spectacular whale watching during the migration season.

Highlights
* Ocean-front building with cliffside pool
* Secure entry, valuable parking on title
* Uninterrupted ocean, bay & cliff views
* Diamond Bay Reserve cliff walk at door
* Spacious and versatile open floorplan
* Wall-to-wall windows, floorboards & tiles
* Separate tidy kitchen with breakfast bar
* Opportunity to add your touches/updates
* Dual-access master bed with built-in robes
* Second bedroom with wall-to-wall windows
* Free Strata Report available at bw.com.au

Corner Block in Blue-Chip Coastal Position Offering Panoramic Views and Huge Potential
29 MACDONALD STREET, VAUCLUSE
Framed by uninterrupted ocean panoramas and bathed in all-day sunshine, this freestanding coastal residence commands a prized north-east facing corner position directly opposite Diamond Bay Reserve. Set on a generous 531sqm parcel, the home is designed to embrace its spectacular clifftop setting, unfolding across expansive interiors with multiple living and entertaining zones, each oriented to capture the sweeping eastern coastline and refreshing sea breezes.

Perfectly configured for large or multi-generational families, the flexible six-bedroom layout offers exceptional space, privacy and versatility, complemented by a north-facing pool and spa, level lawn and internal access to a double lock-up garage. Just moments to Vaucluse village cafés, coastal walks and parklands, this is a home of scale, landholding and lifestyle in one of the suburb's most exclusive pockets.

- Sun-washed interiors with ocean-view formal living and dining zones
- Multiple entertaining terraces with breathtaking, never-to-be-built-out views
- Designer Caesarstone island kitchen with premium European gas appliances
- Expansive family living opening to a sunlit 10m pool and spa
- Six generous bedrooms with built-ins, five bathrooms, three ensuites
- Private master retreat with adjoining study and luxurious spa ensuite
- Lower-level casual living flowing to a level grassed garden
- Flexible floorplan ideal for teen retreat or au pair accommodation
- Self-contained studio with private access, ideal for guests or home office
- Double automatic garage, polished timber floors, alarm and ducted reverse-cycle air-conditioning

STYLISH TOP-FLOOR RETREAT, 200 MTRS FROM OCEAN
12/16 DIAMOND BAY ROAD, VAUCLUSE
Sunny and spacious, this top‑floor apartment offers generous open‑plan living that flows effortlessly to a sun‑splashed balcony that is perfect for relaxing or entertaining.

Just moments from the iconic coastal clifftop walk, this home presents an ideal entry point into sought‑after Vaucluse. Light-filled and inviting, it includes two double bedrooms, an internal laundry, and a bright open‑plan layout designed for modern living.

With nothing to spend, this is an exceptional opportunity for first‑home buyers or astute investors seeking a quality property in a premium location.

Private Contemporary Townhouse, Footsteps to Cafés and Parks
8/765 OLD SOUTH HEAD ROAD, VAUCLUSE
Tucked away to the peaceful rear of a boutique complex and surrounded by parkland, this contemporary townhouse offers a private coastal retreat just moments from Vaucluse village. Bathed in natural light and set across three beautifully designed levels, the home is defined by its sense of seclusion, space, and effortless indoor-outdoor living.

The expansive whole-floor living and dining zone flows seamlessly to a sun-drenched courtyard, creating a tranquil setting for relaxed entertaining. Sleek, streamlined interiors are complemented by polished timber floors and a modern gas kitchen with integrated European appliances.

Accommodation is thoughtfully arranged, with three generous bedrooms featuring built-in wardrobes, including a top-floor retreat with its own balcony. Additional highlights include a mosaic-tiled bathroom with bathtub, powder room, internal laundry, plantation shutters, and abundant storage, including a substantial storeroom.

Offering direct access to secure tandem parking within a tightly held complex of just nine residences, the home is footsteps to coastal cafés and within easy walking distance of boutique harbour beaches, scenic coastal walks to Bondi, and transport options to the city.

• 3 Bed | 1.5 Bath | 2 Car
• Private rear position within a secure complex of 9 residences
• Three double bedrooms with built-in wardrobes
• Top-floor retreat with private balcony
• Open-plan living and dining opening to courtyard
• Streamlined stainless steel gas kitchen with integrated European appliances
• Polished timber floors, reverse-cycle air conditioning
• Mosaic-tiled bathroom with bath, plus powder room
• Internal laundry, plantation shutters, wine cellar/storeroom
• Secure undercover tandem parking plus visitor parking
• Easy access to city buses, minutes to Watsons Bay and ferry

Modern Comfort & Convenience in a Prime Location
3/76-80 MCBURNEY ROAD, CABRAMATTA
An outstanding opportunity for first-home buyers or investors, this well-maintained townhouse is perfectly positioned to enjoy the vibrant lifestyle and amenity rich suburb of Cabramatta.

Welcome to 3/76-80 McBurney Road, Cabramatta — where smart design meets everyday convenience.

Property Features:
+ Spacious Living: Offers two well-proportioned bedrooms — ideal for young professionals, small families, or as a smart investment.
+ Functional Layout: A practical floorplan maximizes space and flow between living areas.

+ Low-Maintenance Living: Perfect for buyers seeking a lock-up-and-leave lifestyle or investors looking for a strong rental prospect.

Convenient Location:
Situated in the heart of Cabramatta, this townhouse offers easy access to local shops, cafes, schools, parks, and essential amenities. Close proximity to public transport makes commuting effortless — with Cabramatta Train Station and bus services just a short distance away.

Perfect for first-home buyers entering the market, downsizers seeking comfort without fuss, or investors looking to capitalise on strong rental demand in this dynamic suburb.

4/73 NEW SOUTH HEAD ROAD, VAUCLUSE
Nestled in one of Sydney's most tightly held enclaves, this elegant three bedroom Art Deco apartment marries period charm with contemporary comfort. Bathed in natural light and set within a boutique block of just six, the residence features high ceilings, original architectural detail and the rare advantage of no common walls, ensuring exceptional privacy and tranquility.

The serene, private ambiance is complemented by effortless access to everyday lifestyle amenities, with Vaucluse Village's cafés, shops and coastal walks just moments away. Offering excellent market appeal for first home buyers, upsizers or investors seeking a classic, blue chip asset, this refined, move-in ready opportunity sits in an address that consistently holds premium appeal.

- Quiet, tightly held block of only 6 apartments

Harbour View Oasis With North Facing Mediterranean Inspired Entertaining
5 MOORE STREET, VAUCLUSE
Evoking the charm of a romantic Mediterranean villa, this luminous harbourside residence is a private sanctuary filled with glorious northerly sunshine. Framed by lush greenery, it enjoys an elevated position with captivating views stretching across the Harbour Bridge and sparkling water. The home delivers exceptional versatility, with a generous lower level recreation zone and multiple light filled living spaces. Tucked away in a tightly held cul-de-sac this home is just a short stroll along a leafy pathway from the golden sands of Gibsons Beach.

• 5 bedrooms, 3 bathrooms, 3 car secure parking
• Beautiful Harbour Bridge and water views
• Gorgeous soaring ceilings, expansive windows
• Atrium style dining room spills onto courtyard
• Elegant marble kitchen, master with ensuite
• Lower level features cinema, vast storage
• Leafy path to Gibsons Beach at end of street
• Stroll to Watsons Bay Village cafés and ferry

Unbeatable Location with Everyday Convenience
13/116 CABRAMATTA ROAD EAST, CABRAMATTA
Bold Real Estate NSW proudly presents a spacious two-bedroom apartment located in the heart of Cabramatta. Perfectly positioned, this home offers convenient access to Cabramatta Station, Cabramatta Mall, local schools, eateries, and everyday amenities, while also being just a short drive to Liverpool and Parramatta CBD.

This property is ideal for first home buyers, downsizers, or investors seeking a low-maintenance lifestyle in a prime location.

Property Features:

+ Generous bedrooms with built-in wardrobes
+ Light-filled living room ideal for entertaining or relaxation
+ Well-appointed kitchen with ample storage spaces
+ Internal laundry with additional storeroom
+ Stylish floorboards throughout, adding a modern touch and offering easy maintenance
+ Secure car space
+ Close proximity to shops, schools, public transport, and all local amenities
+ Easy access to major roads leading to Liverpool, Fairfield, and Parramatta CBD

Location Highlights:

+ Cabramatta Station – approx. 260m
+ Cabramatta Plaza – approx. 550m
+ Cabramatta Primary School – approx. 130m
+ Cabramatta High School – approx. 1km
+ Liverpool CBD – 10 min drive
+ Parramatta CBD – 20 min drive

Contact Bold Real Estate NSW today to arrange a private inspection and experience the lifestyle this apartment has to offer.

Bright & Spacious 2nd Floor Unit - John Street
8/155 JOHN STREET, CABRAMATTA
Perfectly positioned in a well-maintained complex, this light-filled second-floor unit offers comfort, convenience, and lifestyle in one of Cabramatta’s most sought-after locations.

Step inside to discover a generous open-plan living and dining area flowing seamlessly onto a sunny balcony, ideal for relaxing or entertaining. The modern kitchen provides ample storage and bench space, while both bedrooms are well-sized and filled with natural light.

Enjoy the ease of a central location — just moments from Cabramatta’s vibrant shopping precinct, train station, schools, and eateries. Whether you’re a first-home buyer, downsizer, or investor, this is an opportunity not to be missed.

Features:

- Bright and spacious open-plan living and dining area
- Two generous bedrooms with built-in storage potential
- Modern kitchen with plenty of cabinetry
- Sun-drenched balcony with pleasant outlook
- Well-maintained bathroom and internal laundry
- Secure building with huge 27m2 lock up garage
- Prime location – walk to shops, transport, and schools

Location:

- A short stroll to Cabramatta CBD & Train Station
- Close to schools and parks
- A fantastic opportunity for those seeking comfort, convenience, and great value in the heart of Cabramatta.

10/88-92 HUGHES STREET, CABRAMATTA
Perfectly positioned a stroll to Cabramatta Station and the vibrant CBD, this light-filled apartment blends fresh contemporary updates with easy, low-maintenance living. Enjoy morning coffee on one balcony and sunset breezes on the other, plus the rare bonus of a secure lock-up garage.

Highlights

Solid Double Brick Home with Dual Income Potential
56 BOWDEN STREET, CABRAMATTA
Set on a corner block, this double-story, double brick residence is as solid as they come and offers an excellent investment opportunity with dual income potential. The property is separated into two self-contained levels, each with its own access.

Upstairs (Currently Leased at $700 per week):
• Three bedrooms, one bathroom plus two additional toilets
• Lock-up garage with internal entry
• External access for privacy
• Expansive covered balcony, perfect for entertaining

Downstairs (Estimated Rent $650 per week):
• Three bedrooms and one bathroom
• Internal access to the garage
• Direct access to the grassed yard

Rental Potential:
•Combined rental return of approx. $1,350 per week

Ideally located within walking distance to Cabramatta West Public School and Cabramatta High School, and just minutes from Cabramatta Plaza, this property offers both convenience and strong rental appeal.
Whether you’re looking to secure a high-yielding investment or a versatile home with dual living options, this residence presents the perfect opportunity.

Minimalist Oceanfront Luxury With Breathtaking 180-Degree Views In Seidler's Vaucluse Waters
11/33 KIMBERLEY STREET, VAUCLUSE
Suspended above the Pacific with a front-row seat to nature's most dramatic show, this custom-redesigned apartment in Harry Seidler's landmark Vaucluse Waters is immersed in the beauty of nature. A seamless fusion of mid-century modernism and contemporary minimalism, the interiors are pared back to hero the view with walls of glass framing an uninterrupted vista while the ever-changing seascape brings an uplifting sense of calm and connection. Fully renovated with Seidler's signature clean lines and functional flow, the oversized layout creates the sensation of floating above the ocean with the sounds of the sea underscoring a mood of serenity. Entertain against an infinite blue backdrop or unwind by the pool perched on the cliff's edge with level lift access to secure parking for effortless living. Step outside to the Diamond Bay Cliff Walk leading to Watsons Bay or stroll to The Grumpy Baker and Christison Park. This is coastal living at its most iconic and inspiring with views that will never be built out just 700m to Coles Local and Rose Bay North village.

- Mid-century landmark built in 1960
- Anchored to the Diamond Bay coast
- Second floor with level lift access
- Parking on title, video intercom
- East facing with unobstructed views
- Large bedroom with built-in robes
- Streamlined Euro-appointed kitchen
- Glass-framed living and dining
- Clean lines and organic curves
- Designer bathroom, Grohe tapware
- Internal laundry and C Bus lighting
- 15m heated pool on the ocean's edge
- Direct access to the coastal boardwalk
- Walk along the cliffs to Watsons Bay
- 700m to village life and Coles Local
- Mins to idyllic harbour and surf beaches

Property Description
SOLD BY MESHEL BAHNAM ! 0478 664 123
Meshel Bahnam of the Ray White United Group is proud to present to the market 20A Derwent Street, Mount Druitt !

If you've been searching for a home that offers room for the whole family without compromising on convenience, 20A Derwent Street, Mount Druitt is one you won't want to miss. Beautifully presented and thoughtfully designed, this spacious residence delivers the flexibility and functionality that modern families are looking for.

From the moment you step inside, you'll appreciate the generous proportions and light-filled interiors. The open-plan living and dining areas create a welcoming atmosphere, while the kitchen is perfectly positioned to keep you connected with family and guests. With four well-sized bedrooms, each featuring built-in robes, there's plenty of space for everyone, while the master bedroom enjoys the added privacy of its own ensuite.

Outside, the patio offers a great place to enjoy your morning coffee, host weekend barbecues, or simply unwind after a long day. Automatic remote-controlled garage, handy shed, internal laundry, and low-maintenance yard complete a home that's designed for easy everyday living.

Whether you're upsizing, accommodating extended family, or looking for a quality investment, this home offers the space, comfort, and location to suit a wide range of buyers. Move in and enjoy everything this fantastic Mount Druitt address has to offer.

WHAT WE LOVE ABOUT THIS PROPERTY:
*4 spacious bedrooms with built-in robes, including a master with ensuite
*3 well-appointed bathrooms
*Light-filled living and dining areas
*Well-equipped kitchen with ample storage
*Internal laundry
*Patio for year-round entertaining
*Welcoming front porch
*Automatic remote-controlled garage
*Backyard shed for extra storage
*Double-storey home with ground and first-floor living

SOME LOCAL AMENITIES:
*Mount Druitt Public School - Approx. 650m
*RAAF Memorial Park - Approx. 750m
*Westfield Mt Druitt - Approx. 1.0km
*Whalan Public School - Approx. 1.3km

Property Description
Timeless Elegance and Refined Luxury with Panoramic City & Harbour Bridge Vistas
Beyond its exquisite historic façade and captivating street presence, this exceptional Vaucluse residence unveils a world of refined grandeur and contemporary luxury in a prestigious blue-ribbon address.

Tightly-held by the one family for 29 years and occupying an elevated 967sqm parcel, this impeccably appointed home delivers an extraordinary sense of arrival, framed by beautiful established gardens and enjoying panoramic vistas towards the city skyline and Harbour Bridge.

Impressive in both scale and design, it reveals elegant formal lounge and dining rooms beneath soaring 3.6m ornate ceilings, where a premium gas fireplace and timeless detailing create an atmosphere of warmth and refinement.

At the heart of the home is a gourmet island kitchen equipped with premium Miele gas appliances, unfolding into a to a sun washed family domain designed for relaxed everyday living and entertaining.

From here, effortless transitions lead to a private resort-style backyard with a mosaic-tiled swimming pool and spa embraced by lush manicured gardens, while a superb poolside cabana complete with a fully-integrated outdoor kitchen and BBQ deliver the ultimate setting for year-round entertaining.

Accommodation comprises four generous bedrooms appointed with built-in wardrobes, three of which are positioned on the lower level. The upstairs master is a true sanctuary, complete with a deluxe ensuite, while opening to a sun bathed balcony capturing breathtaking views to the city skyline and harbour.

All beautifully appointed, the three Travertine bathrooms feature underfloor heating, while there is a chic guest powder room and a separate family sized laundry enjoying direct access to a secluded drying deck.

Further highlights of the home include a custom study, a 2,000-bottle climate-controlled wine cellar, ducted air conditioning and integrated sound.

Complete with a double remote garage and plenty of off-street parking, this graceful property is located just minutes to Vaucluse and Rose Bay shopping villages, elite schools, city transport and boutique harbour beaches.

- 4 bed | 3.5 bath | 4 car
- Historic Vaucluse residence built in 1926 on 967sqm parcel
- Elevated setting to capture views to the ocean, city & harbour
- Grand proportions with both formal/informal living & dining
- Elegant formal lounge and dining with soaring 3.6m ceilings
- Cleverly designed with skylights drawing in abundant sunlight
- Gourmet kitchen with Miele gas appliances and two ovens
- Integrated Miele dishwasher, Qaisar rangehood, ample storage
- Resort-style backyard with sparkling mosaic-tiled pool & spa
- Poolside entertainers' cabana w/ integrated kitchen & BBQ
- Beautiful established front and rear landscaped gardens
- Custom study, four generous bedrooms with built-in wardrobes
- Upstairs master sanctuary with full-sized designer ensuite
- Master with terrace and magical city & Harbour Bridge views
- Three deluxe Travertine bathrooms with underfloor heating
- Chic guest powder room, all with Albert Adams tapware
- 2,000 bottle climate-controlled wine cellar, AV intercom
- Zoned ducted a/c, integrated sound, Heat&Glo gas fireplace
- Double remote garage with additional off-street parking
- Family-sized laundry with direct access to drying deck
- Exquisite street presence, original sandstone foundation
- Walk to prestigious schools, buses, Vaucluse shopping village
- Moments to cosmopolitan Rose Bay village, harbour beaches

In conjunction with Steven Zoellner | Laing+Simmons, Double Bay - 0400 840 111 steven.zoellner@lsre.com.au

Property Description
A Landmark Luxury Residence of Palatial Scale in the Heart of Cabramatta
Commanding attention from the moment you arrive, this grand luxury residence delivers a rare combination of palatial proportions, uncompromising craftsmanship and enduring full double brick and concrete construction all set on an expansive parcel of over 1,200m².

Designed for buyers who demand space, prestige and permanence, the home showcases an impressive sense of scale throughout, with grand living domains, refined architectural detail and a level of construction rarely found in modern residences. Every element speaks to quality, from its solid foundations to its carefully considered finishes, creating a home built not only for today's luxury lifestyle but for generations to come.

Positioned only a short distance from Cabramatta CBD and train station, this exceptional property offers the perfect balance of privacy, convenience and status. Enjoy effortless access to shopping, dining, transport and essential amenities while retreating to a residence that feels worlds apart in size, presence and distinction.

Set on substantial land, the property also presents outstanding potential for those seeking a prestigious family estate, multi-generational living or a blue-chip holding in one of the area's most connected locations.

Property highlights:

- Grand luxury home with commanding street presence
- Palatial proportions and expansive living spaces
- Unrivalled craftsmanship and enduring quality
- Full double brick and concrete construction
- Set on over 1,200m² of premium land
- Moments to Cabramatta CBD, train station, shops and dining
- Ideal for luxury buyers, large families and prestige investors

A residence of this calibre is seldom offered grand in scale, solid in construction and superbly positioned for lifestyle and legacy.
