Property Description
Top Floor Bespoke Coastal Sanctuary with Sweeping Ocean Views
Capturing a sweeping ocean panorama that stretches across the coastline to the horizon, this exquisite custom apartment occupies the entire eastern wing of a tightly-held well-maintained building in a prized Vaucluse address.

Transformed by a comprehensive bespoke renovation with no expense spared, interiors are defined by a sleek and meticulously considered material palette that elevates every surface. Recycled timber elements and European oak floors introduce natural warmth, balanced by the sculptural softness of Venetian plaster walls bringing depth, texture and a quiet sense of luxury.

Cleverly designed with two separate living zones and ocean views from almost every room, it reveals an impressive layout with both front and rear sun soaked view-swept balconies, perfect for year-round entertaining and allowing effortless cross-flow.

A north facing light filled lounge is warmed in winter by a premium electric cooktop, while a state-of-the-art kitchen features quality Caesarstone benchtops and an array of European appliances including an induction cooktop and an integrated dishwasher.

The three beautifully appointed bedrooms are designed as relaxing, light filled retreats, all of which feature built-in wardrobes. Both the main bathroom and master ensuite are stylishly appointed with rainwater showers, Parisi tapware and underfloor heating, while additional features include a concealed internal laundry, zoned ducted air conditioning, designer lighting and Smart home technology.

Residents enjoy access to the complex beautiful communal gardens and a newly refurbished swimming pool plus a lock-up garage with additional storage.

This luxury apartment is located within a stroll of Diamond Bay Reserve and breathtaking oceanfront parklands as well as local cafés, city buses and Vaucluse village shopping and dining strip. Rose Bay North village with Coles and specialty stores are just minutes away, as is Watsons Bay, prestigious schools and a series of boutique harbour beaches.

- 3 bed | 2 bath | 1car
- Top floor and occupies entire eastern wing of the building
- Magnificent, elevated ocean panorama stretching to horizon
- Transformed by custom renovation with no expense spared
- Impressive flowing layout featuring two separate living zones
- Cleverly designed with two balconies to enjoy easy cross-flow
- State-of-the-art Caesarstone kitchen, Bosch induction cooktop
- Premium European appliances, Gessi hot/cold tap, dishwasher
- Breathtaking ocean views from almost every room of apartment
- Superb wraparound view-swept balcony with automated awning
- Second n/facing sun soaked balcony w/ district/ocean views
- Zoned ducted air conditioning, wide European oak flooring
- Smart home technology, extensive storage, sheer curtains
- Deluxe bathrooms with underfloor heating, Parisi tapware
- Both bathrooms with rainwater showers and Smart toilets
- Venetian plaster wall with an integrated electric fireplace
- Recycled timber, slatted timber vertical door to int. laundry
- Carrara marble tiled floor & exposed concrete in kitchen
- Single lock-up garage, communal gardens, refurbished pool
- One min stroll to Diamond Bay Reserve, clifftop walkways
- Walk to popular local cafés, buses, Vaucluse village shops
- Minutes to Rose Bay North Village, Coles, esteemed schools
- Close to boutique harbour beaches, parklands, Watsons Bay

Property Description
'Virginia' a timeless residence with breathtaking harbour views
Capturing mesmerising uninterrupted views across the harbour to the Bridge and Opera House, this gracious residence stands as a charming example of the elegant architecture of its time.

Positioned on an 882sqm parcel of land, it retains fine period detailing and grand proportions, encompassing an abundance of accommodation and entertaining spaces. Comfortable and rich in character, the home lends itself to reinterpretation and enhancement of its original fabric, creating a residence befitting its world-class location.

A brief stroll will take you to the picturesque shores of Nielsen Park, secluded Hermit Beach and the scenic Hermitage Foreshore Walk that meanders around to Watsons Bay and Camp Cove.

Key Features Indoor:
Five bedrooms including huge master suite and private guest room
Grand formal lounge with wood fire, stately formal dining room
Colonnaded glass-embraced casual sitting and dining room
Upper-level study, Caesarstone gas kitchen with breakfast bar

Key Features Outdoor:
Breathtaking views to the icons of the city skyline
Sandstone-flagged patio & pool bathed in afternoon sun
Front porch, established gardens surrounding the residence
LUG plus sandstone driveway accommodating tandem vehicles

Key Features Additional:
Intricately detailed timber wall panelling, hardwood floorboards

With gas cooking, built-in wardrobes in both bedrooms, and split-system air conditioning, this property provides a comfortable lifestyle with everything you need close by.

• Close to a selection of local primary and secondary schools, childcare centres, and educational facilities
• Nearby parks, playgrounds, and recreational spaces ideal for families and outdoor activities
• Easy access to major arterial roads including the Great Western Highway and the M4 Motorway for convenient commuting

Step outside and discover the ultimate entertainer's retreat featuring a sparkling swimming pool and stylish cabana, perfect for hosting family gatherings, weekend BBQs or simply relaxing in your own resort-style setting.

Conveniently located close to local schools, shops, parks and public transport, this brand new residence offers the perfect combination of luxury, lifestyle and location in one of Cabramatta's most vibrant communities.

Property Description
European Charm with Exceptional Land Potential!
A wonderful example of the finest craftsmanship, this sensational and privately constructed full brick family residence enjoys a seamless integration between indoor and outdoor living, with attention to detail and privacy being paramount. First time offered to market, this charming family abode embraces the elegant original features with oversized interiors providing welcome additions for modern living. Boasting an expansive single level layout, it provides a flexible and comfortable design suitable for the multigenerational family or even a possible dual occupancy arrangement.

Featuring a high desirable sized parcel of approximately 1,012sqm it also provides the savvy and sophisticated buyer with multiple investment and future development options with value adding opportunities to further improve rental income and potential future growth. Presenting immense opportunities and flexibility to renovate, redesign or rebuild, it allows buyers the potential for an architecturally designed residence, dual occupancy living or multiple dwellings, granny flat addition or even an ideal home business arrangement (S.T.C.A.).

Peacefully nestled within a family friendly setting and amongst other quality designer residences, this beautifully appointed family home features bright interiors enhanced by soaring high ceilings, well proportioned living spaces and an inviting European charm which flows effortlessly throughout. Perfectly orientated to capture the eastern sun this stunning residence provides immediate access to all essential amenities including but not limited to Cabramatta Station, Cabramatta Bowling Club, Cabramatta Public School and the vibrant Cabramatta CBD offering an endless selection of restaurants, cafes and fresh produce all year round!

Property Features:
- Privately constructed single level full brick family residence.
- Well proportioned two (2) bedrooms with potential for 3-bedroom conversion.
- Master bedroom includes private and spacious en-suite.
- Original kitchen and main bathroom with scope for modern improvements.
- Bright interiors providing separate living and dining spaces ideal for endless entertaining.
- Beautifully appointed alfresco with an outdoor integrated BBQ.
- Additional large detached rear dwelling offering a double lock up garage with workshop and teenagers retreat/sleepout with private bathroom.
- Beautifully manicured gardens throughout
- Private and secure fence with remote controlled gate and wide driveway access to rear.

Site Features:
- Zone R2: Low Density Residential
- Land size: 1,012sqm ( approximate )
- Dimensions: 20.11m x 50.29m ( approximate )
- Potential dual occupancy/granny flat development (S.T.C.A.)

RARE GROUND-FLOOR IN THE HEART OF CABRAMATTA!
Rare Ground-Floor Opportunity in the Heart of Cabramatta CBD
Seize this rare opportunity to secure a spacious ground-floor three-bedroom unit in the vibrant heart of Cabramatta CBD. Perfectly positioned just moments from local amenities, restaurants, cafes, the train station, and Sacred Heart Catholic Primary School, this home offers outstanding convenience and lifestyle appeal.
Ideal for downsizers, young families, or savvy investors, this well-appointed residence combines comfort, accessibility, and a highly sought-after location.
Property Features:
+ Three generous bedrooms with built-in wardrobes
+ Master bedroom with private ensuite
+ Light-filled open-plan living and dining area
+ Modern kitchen with ample storage and bench space
+ Ground-floor position ideal for young families and seniors
+ Two split-system air conditioning units
+ Designated parking space
Enjoy the convenience of living within walking distance to shops, eateries, transport, and schools in one of South-West Sydney's most vibrant precincts.
With its prime location and rare ground-floor positioning, this exceptional home is an opportunity not to be missed. Contact us today to arrange an inspection.

Beautifully appointed townhouse in `Vaucluse Gardens'
18/31-39 DIAMOND BAY ROAD (ACCESS VIA ISABEL AVE), VAUCLUSE
Peacefully positioned in the sought-after `Vaucluse Gardens' complex, this stylishly renovated townhouse delivers an idyllic coastal sanctuary within a stroll of breathtaking Diamond Bay Reserve. Cleverly designed for contemporary comfort and ease of living with dual aspects and an effortless cross-flow, it features a wonderfully spacious layout with a crisp coastal aesthetic enhanced by engineered timber floors and fresh neutral tones. A gourmet island kitchen is equipped with premium gas appliances, while open plan living and dining areas transition seamlessly to front and rear sun washed courtyards. The upper-level comprises two well-sized bedrooms, both of which are appointed with built-in wardrobes and reverse cycle air conditioning. The main bedroom features a chic skylit ensuite and opens to an undercover balcony enjoying gentle sea breezes. Further highlights include a stylish full-sized main bathroom and pull-down attic storage with potential for a loft-style conversion. Residents enjoy access to the complex beautifully landscaped gardens and a swimming pool plus a secure basement car space. It is exceptionally located just minutes to Rose Bay North village shops, Coles and popular cafés as well as Vaucluse Village, Watsons Bay and within footsteps to buses.

Features:
• Tastefully renovated for contemporary living
• Beautifully presented with stylish neutral tones
• Front and rear courtyards, effortless cross-flow
• North facing rear courtyard awash with sunshine
• Gourmet island kitchen, premium gas appliances
• Breakfast bar, ample cupboard storage, dishwasher
• Well-sized bedrooms appointed with built-in robes
• Both bedrooms with reverse cycle air conditioning
• Modern 2.5 bathrooms, main with bath and shower
• Main with stylish ensuite and a covered balcony
• Lower level third w/c, ceiling fans, study nook
• Residents access complex gardens and pool
• Pull-down attic/scope for loft-style conversion
• Convenient access to secure basement car space
• Steps to Diamond Bay Reserve and coastal walk
• Minutes to Rose Bay North village shops, Coles
• Close to Vaucluse village, buses, popular cafés
• Moments to premier schools and Watsons Bay

Sold By Ty Demirezen 0422 577 720
1/697 OLD SOUTH HEAD ROAD, VAUCLUSE
This property has now sold - For more information, please call Ty Demirezen on 0422 577 720.

Space, style and light - family-sized apartment by Diamond Bay
Three-bedroom apartments in Vaucluse are a sought-after commodity and this stylishly revamped coastal residence ticks every box. On the first floor of a recently upgraded boutique building of just six, it's spacious, tastefully appointed and bathed in sunshine, capturing district views and ocean glimpses from its balcony. Generous entertaining space, an as-new designer kitchen and bathroom, an oversized lock-up garage and air-conditioning make this apartment wonderfully liveable and appealing. Enjoy Kimberley Reserve as your playground, take a leisurely stroll along the revitalised Diamond Bay Cliffwalk or wander to Vaucluse village shops and beloved local eateries such as Botanica, Rocco's and Woshoku. For an easy commute, buses to the city and Watsons Bay are just footsteps from your door.

- Three bedrooms, two with b/ins, including king-sized main.
- Roomy living and dining space opening to a sunny balcony.
- Contemporary CaesarStone kitchen with Euro appliances.
- Stylish bathroom with a bath and double stone vanity.
- Guest powder room, integrated laundry in the kitchen.
- Windows on three sides with no common walls for privacy.
- Timber floors, integrated bookshelves, plantation shutters.
- Oversized lock-up garage with integrated storage space.
- Daikin reverse cycle air cond for year-round comfort.
- Follow the Gap Bluff Walking Track to Watsons Bay.
- 350m to Rose Bay North village and Coles supermarket.
- Catchment for highly regarded Vaucluse Public School.

Stunning Family Entertainer On Expansive Vaucluse Block With Boundless Potential
205 HOPETOUN AVENUE, VAUCLUSE
Positioned upon a vast 750 sqm block, elevated for exceptional privacy and beautiful views, this brilliant family home offers superb poolside entertaining and an abundance of future potential. Facing north, it is designed to capture vibrant natural light at all hours of the day, epitomised by the sun-washed living and dining spaces and the remarkable array of outdoor entertaining areas. The picturesque front terrace and verandah offer an idyllic setting with a magnificent backdrop for dinner parties, while the rear garden provides a large swimming pool and numerous poolside settings to enjoy throughout the day. With grand proportions throughout, the home is ready to enjoy while demonstrating incredible renovation potential, as well as the scope to build designer duplexes (STCA). It is positioned footsteps from Gibsons Beach Reserve and a short stroll from Watsons Bay ferries.

Property Features:
- North-facing living & dining with stunning width & volume
- Spectacular outdoor entertaining & vibrant poolside dining
- Breathtaking north-facing balcony with Watsons Bay views
- Updated open kitchen with Miele induction & two ovens
- Oversized master suite with a walk-in & an ensuite spa bath
- Children's wing includes a media room & 3 beds with built-ins
- Ground-floor guest powder room, additional WC in laundry
- Charming sandstone façade with a double automatic garage
- Potential to renovate or rebuild luxurious duplexes (STCA)
- Enjoy a morning stroll on the serene Watsons Bay waterfront
- Walking distance to Vaucluse Public School, buses to the CBD

322 Military Road, Vaucluse NSW 2030

Shortlist
Property Description
Coastal Family Entertainer Offers the Ultimate Layout.
Elevated to capture sweeping ocean views in a prime Vaucluse address, this exceptional freestanding residence delivers an extraordinary standard of family living and entertaining across an impressive tri-level design.

A welcoming entrance foyer draws you into the home's expansive living domain, where generous lounge and dining areas create a strong sense of openness and an effortless connection to the outdoors.

The kitchen is a statement in understated elegance while also a high-performance workspace, featuring deluxe Calacatta marble benchtops including a vast island breakfast bar and premium Miele gas appliances.

Seamless transitions are made to a fabulous alfresco entertaining setting with a Vergola, while beyond the experience becomes distinctly resort-like. An elevated sun bathed swimming pool and an adjoining cabana amid lush gardens give relaxed holiday vibes and a true sense of escape.

Accommodation comprises four well-proportioned bedrooms on the upper-level, all of which are appointed with built-in wardrobes and ceiling fans, while both the master and adjoining living area open directly to a sunlit full-width terrace with superb ocean views.

Entertainment excellence continues with an incredible lower-level living space featuring a family rumpus/games room and media zone, while further highlights include ducted air conditioning, C-bus home automation, an alarm system and extensive storage.

Finishes of rich timber, glass and Travertine are showcased throughout, while the residence is complete with internal access to a double remote garage plus additional off-street parking.

Its premier location is within a stroll of breathtaking oceanfront parklands and clifftop boardwalks as well as Rose Bay North with buses, village shops and eateries, Coles and everyday conveniences. It is just minutes to Vaucluse village and prestigious schools as well as boutique harbour beaches and Watsons Bay.

- 4 bed | 3 bath | 3 car
- Impressive open plan living/dining with level flow to rear
- Incredible chef's Calacatta marble kitchen w/ island b/bar
- Premium Miele gas appliances, abundant pantry storage
- Seamless transition to entertainers' terrace with Vergola
- Elevated pool with cabana in sun bathed garden surrounds
- Curved staircase w/ circular skylight leads to upper-level
- Upper-level bedrooms with built-in robes and ceiling fans
- Master with walk-in wardrobe and chic fully tiled ensuite
- Designer marble bathrooms, main with corner spa bath
- Master & upper-living flow to full-width ocean view terrace
- Expansive rumpus/family room & media area on lower level
- Exquisite finishes, rich timber and Travertine throughout
- Zoned ducted air conditioning, C-bus home automation
- Polished timber floors on upper-level, extensive storage
- Lower-level features concrete flooring, alarm system
- Internal access to double garage, off street parking
- Walk to Vaucluse village, eateries, everyday conveniences

44A Wentworth Road, Vaucluse NSW 2030

Property Description
Modernist Architectural Residence With Dual Frontages, Pool & Breathtaking Views To Harbour Bridge And Opera House
In a blue-chip position opposite harbourfront parkland and the foreshore walk to Neilsen Park, this contemporary family residence stands on approx. 680 sqm land with frontages to Wentworth Road and Vaucluse Road, two of the most prestigious addresses in Vaucluse. Superbly reimagined by celebrated architect James Grose, the home impresses with light-filled interiors, exceptional privacy, leafy surrounds and spectacular harbour views to the Opera House, Harbour Bridge and city skyline.

A timeless modernist aesthetic defines the five-bedroom house created for indoor-outdoor living and entertaining with seamless flow to spacious covered alfresco areas on both levels. Of superior quality and construction with wall-to-wall glass maximising natural light and harbour views over two spacious levels with living and dining areas, gourmet kitchen, master bedroom suite, study (or bedroom) and powder room at entry level, while downstairs is ideal for teenagers with 3 bedrooms, 2 bathrooms, large home office and a second living area flowing to the poolside terrace, pool and covered terrace.

Enjoy relaxed luxury and greenery all around in this tranquil home with north side aspect, ducted air-con, CCTV security, solar roof panels and secure entry from the garage to a ramp leading to the main level, as well as stairs to the lower level with its own entrance. A rare find with only two owners since the original c1950s house was built, the property presents an enviable lifestyle with footsteps to harbour beaches, minutes to Vaucluse shopping village and close proximity to excellent schools.

- Spectacular harbour views to Opera House and Harbour Bridge
- Directly opposite parkland surrounding historic Strickland House
- Entrance hall with wide pivot door & custom built-in bookshelves
- Living area with gas fireplace, wall-to-wall glass & harbour views
- Dining area with custom built-in bookshelves & harbour views
- Covered alfresco deck with space to seat 12 or more people
- Second large living area (lower level) flows to pool & terraces
- Large gourmet kitchen with stone benchtops & walk-in pantry
- Miele induction cooktop & dishwasher, Neff oven & steam oven
- Qasair stainless steel rangehood, generous storage, wine fridge
- Bifold doors allow the kitchen to be closed from the dining area
- Master king-size bedroom with walk-in wardrobe and ensuite
- Master ensuite with bath, separate shower, twin basin vanity
- Study (or 2nd bedroom) with custom built-in bookshelves
- 3 more bedrooms (lower level) with b/ins, one with ensuite
- 3 bathrooms, powder room, laundry with sink and storage
- Covered alfresco courtyard on lower level (ideal for teenagers)
- Laundry opens to drying courtyard with Hills Hoist clothesline
- Separate storage room with access from the drying courtyard
- High ceilings, polished timber floors, sunny north side aspect
- Ducted r/c air-conditioning, solar roof panels, CCTV security
- 2 car auto-door garage with secure access to entrance ramp
- 220m to harbourside Milk Beach & Hermitage Foreshore Walk
- 100m to Neilsen Park & 500m to harbourside Shark Beach
- Minutes to Vaucluse shopping village, close to top schools

35 Cambridge Avenue, Vaucluse NSW 2030

Shortlist
Property Description
Brand-New Architectural Family Entertainer of Visionary Design and Superior Craftsmanship
Visionary design, state-of-the-art finishes and exceptional craftsmanship define this newly built architectural residence, delivering a home of remarkable quality and striking individuality.

Designed with elegant curves, soaring ceilings and a refined yet durable material palette, this four-bedroom residence has been conceived as both a luxurious family sanctuary and an impressive entertainer. A seamless level flow connects the interiors to a sun-soaked outdoor oasis where a heated quad-jet swimming pool forms the centrepiece of the home's private entertaining space.

Spanning two striking levels and bathed in natural light, the home reveals a chef's marble island kitchen at its heart, appointed with premium Wolf gas appliances and a fully equipped butler's pantry. Expansive open-plan living and dining areas are anchored by a statement gas fireplace and transition effortlessly to the outdoor entertaining area.

Accommodation is thoughtfully arranged across the upper level with three generous bedrooms, each featuring custom built-in wardrobes, ceiling fans and beautifully appointed ensuites. The luxurious master suite enjoys a boutique-style dressing room/walk-in wardrobe and a designer ensuite.

All four marble bathrooms are elegantly finished with rain showers and quality Caroma tapware, while the master ensuite also features a freestanding bath and dual vanity. Additional highlights include a private lift servicing both levels, extensive custom joinery, a lower-level guest bedroom and zoned reverse-cycle air conditioning for year-round comfort.

Framed by beautifully landscaped gardens and accessed via a Summit cobblestone driveway with carport and additional off-street parking, this exceptional residence is positioned just metres from Vaucluse Public School and moments to Kutti Beach, connecting to the scenic harbourfront walk to Gibsons Beach, Watsons Bay and Camp Cove.

Ray White Double Bay - Exceptional Living Begins Here

Our recommended loan broker https://broker.loanmarket.com.au/DOUBLE-BAY

Spanning two striking levels and bathed in natural light, the home reveals a chef's marble island kitchen at its heart, appointed with premium Wolf gas appliances and a fully equipped butler's pantry. Expansive open-plan living and dining areas are anchored by a statement gas fireplace and transition effortlessly to the outdoor entertaining area.

Accommodation is thoughtfully arranged across the upper level with three generous bedrooms, each featuring custom built-in wardrobes, ceiling fans and beautifully appointed ensuites. The luxurious master suite enjoys a boutique-style dressing room/walk-in wardrobe and a designer ensuite.

All four marble bathrooms are elegantly finished with rain showers and quality Caroma tapware, while the master ensuite also features a freestanding bath and dual vanity. Additional highlights include a private lift servicing both levels, extensive custom joinery, a lower-level guest bedroom and zoned reverse-cycle air conditioning for year-round comfort.

Framed by beautifully landscaped gardens and accessed via a Summit cobblestone driveway with carport and additional off-street parking, this exceptional residence is positioned just metres from Vaucluse Public School and moments to Kutti Beach, connecting to the scenic harbourfront walk to Gibsons Beach, Watsons Bay and Camp Cove.

Ray White Double Bay - Exceptional Living Begins Here

Property Description
Contemporary Harbour-Side Retreat
Positioned just 120m from Vaucluse Public School and an easy 700m stroll to Parsley Bay Beach and Reserve, this exceptional residence pairs architectural pedigree with effortless coastal living. Once a humble fisherman's cottage, it has been masterfully reimagined into a sophisticated contemporary sanctuary where industrial elements meet refined finishes. Designed to celebrate natural light, the home reveals soaring volumes, expansive glazing and strategically placed skylights that flood the interiors with warmth throughout the day. Privately set yet moments to both Vaucluse and Watsons Bay villages, it delivers a rare combination of design excellence, convenience and lifestyle appeal.

- Secure gated off-street parking plus DA approval for a future carport seamlessly integrated with the façade
- Architecturally transformed by renowned designer Ed Lippmann
- Striking double-height voids enhancing space and natural light
- Expansive open-plan living and dining zones across a full entertaining level
- Three generous bedrooms, including a master retreat with ensuite and walk-in robe
- Designer gas kitchen featuring stainless steel finishes and a movable marble island
- North-facing courtyard oasis with mosaic-tiled pool, sun lounge and integrated BBQ/wet bar
- Premium materials throughout including Blackbutt timber floors and Carrara marble
- Curated Italian lighting complementing the home's contemporary aesthetic

Bathed in natural light from its sunny north-east aspect, the bright and airy three-bedroom layout flows seamlessly to its indoor/outdoor entertaining zones, offering superb comfort and flexibility for young families and downsizers alike. Located less than 200 metres to Kimberley and Diamond Bay Reserves, with scenic clifftop walks and village conveniences just moments away, it perfectly balances lifestyle and location

Property Description
The Ultimate Family Entertainer.
Nestled amid lush tropical gardens with a prized north-east facing level backyard, this exceptional multi-level residence delivers the ultimate environment for luxurious family living and entertaining in a premier Vaucluse address.

Recently refreshed and flooded with natural light, it features a thoughtfully considered layout unfolding effortlessly from front-to-rear, through a series of living and dining zones framed by floor-to-ceiling glass complemented by warm timber finishes and striking sandstone accents.

A vast sequence of superb sun soaked alfresco spaces extend the living areas to the outdoors, with an all-weather entertaining deck overlooking a private, child-friendly level yard with a sparkling infinity swimming pool and spa offering an idyllic setting for relaxed family life and weekend gatherings.

Chef's will delight in the gourmet stone kitchen equipped with a full suite of Gaggenau gas appliances and a butler's pantry, while the upper-level is dedicated to accommodation with five well-sized bedrooms designed as relaxing, light filled retreats. All five bedrooms are appointed with built-in wardrobes, four of which feature custom built-in desks and the luxurious master features a chic ensuite with dual vanity, a standalone bath and rainwater shower plus access to a private sunny balcony overlooking the secluded backyard.

The lower levels are equally impressive with a private custom study, a large media/rumpus room and a wine cellar, creating versatile spaces for work, play and entertaining.

Further highlights include zoned ducted air conditioning, a premium gas fireplace, extensive internal storage, a family sized laundry with direct outdoor access and internal access to an oversized double remote garage.

This impressive family residence is positioned within a stroll of Parsley Bay Beach and Vaucluse Public School, while moments to Vaucluse village, boutique harbour beaches, public transport and prestigious schools.

- 5 bed + study | 4 bath | 2 car
- Impressive proportions w/ a sequence of living/dining zones
- Recently refreshed throughout and bathed in natural light
- Upper level enjoys sweeping leafy views towards the harbour
- Interiors flow to a vast series of sun soaked alfresco spaces
- North east facing level backyard amid lush tropical gardens
- Covered all-weather entertainment deck with integrated BBQ
- Infinity swimming pool and spa with tranquil water features
- Gourmet stone kitchen with premium Gaggenau gas appliances
- Full butler's pantry, island breakfast bar, Vintec wine fridge
- Welcoming lounge, premium gas fireplace, custom cabinetry
- Five well-sized upper-level bedrooms with built-in wardrobes
- Luxe master bedroom with designer ensuite & sunlit balcony
- Lower-level media room, wine cellar, stylish bathrooms
- Main bath & ensuite w/ standalone bath, rainwater shower
- Private study with built-in desks and extensive storage
- Ducted air conditioning, timber floorboards, sandstone wall
- Internal access to oversized double garage, vast storage
- Solid double brick and concrete construction foundation
- Stroll to Parsley Bay Beach and Vaucluse Public School
- Short walk to Kutti Beach, Vaucluse Bay and Nielsen Park
- Minutes to Vaucluse village shops, cafés, specialty stores
- Easy access to prestigious schools, Watsons Bay, transport

Framed by uninterrupted ocean panoramas and bathed in all-day sunshine, this freestanding coastal residence commands a prized north-east facing corner position directly opposite Diamond Bay Reserve. Set on a generous 531sqm parcel, the home is designed to embrace its spectacular clifftop setting, unfolding across expansive interiors with multiple living and entertaining zones, each oriented to capture the sweeping eastern coastline and refreshing sea breezes.

Perfectly configured for large or multi-generational families, the flexible six-bedroom layout offers exceptional space, privacy and versatility, complemented by a north-facing pool and spa, level lawn and internal access to a double lock-up garage. Just moments to Vaucluse village cafés, coastal walks and parklands, this is a home of scale, landholding and lifestyle in one of the suburb's most exclusive pockets.

Property Description
Perfect for down-sizers. Lock up and go in a modern boutique block
This modern apartment offers stylish finishes, generous proportions, and a practical layout designed for relaxed coastal living. Located within a boutique development, it features open-plan living and dining that connects seamlessly to a private balcony, ideal for entertaining or unwinding.

The sleek gas kitchen is fitted with quality integrated appliances, including a fridge and dishwasher, and ample storage throughout. Both bedrooms include built-in wardrobes, with the main featuring a private ensuite. A second bathroom includes a full-sized bathtub, and there's also an internal laundry.

Ducted air conditioning provides year-round comfort, completing a home that balances low-maintenance living with premium design.

Set just moments from Vaucluse village, harbour beaches, and scenic coastal walks, this apartment delivers easy access to some of the East's most desirable lifestyle attractions.

• Open-plan living flowing to private balcony
• Designer gas kitchen with integrated fridge & dishwasher
• Two bedrooms with built-in wardrobes
• Main bedroom with private ensuite
• Second bathroom with full-sized bathtub
• Ducted air conditioning and internal laundry

UNDER CONTRACT BY MATT DEBONO 0432 443 770
10 FRANCIS STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
Matt DeBono from Harcourts Your Place is excited to present 10 Francis Street, Mount Druitt - a home that has not been offered to the market in over 52 years! Featuring four bedrooms, two bathrooms, and two living areas, and positioned in a prime location, this home is a perfect example of an exceptionally maintained and updated family residence. Sitting on a massive 763sqm block, this property also offers granny flat potential (STCA).

Stepping inside, you are greeted by:

- A large lounge room with LED downlights
- Four sizable bedrooms with ceiling fans, perfect for growing families
- Three bedrooms containing built-in wardrobes
- A large master bedroom featuring a stunning ensuite to match the main bathroom, plus porch access
- Loads of linen storage and an additional toilet in the hallway
- An open-plan family, dining, and kitchen area
- A stunning internal laundry with the tub integrated into the benchtops and a broom closet
- A separate powder room off the laundry

The renovated kitchen features:

- Ample cupboard space
- 40mm solid surface benchtops with an integrated deep double-bowl sink, adding a touch of class
- Top-of-the-range Miele appliances - oven, stove, electric cooktop, rangehood, and integrated dishwasher
- A sleek glass splashback
- Tiled flooring
- Soft-close doors
- Plumbing behind the fridge, ready to go

The stunning main bathroom includes:

- Floor-to-ceiling tiles
- A beautiful freestanding bath
- Floating vanity featuring solid surface benchtops and an integrated bowl
- A massive frameless mirror
- A large frameless shower
- Wall-mounted TV

Stepping outside, this is your own private oasis - offering space for entertaining while the kids run around in complete privacy.

- A certified 8-10 person heated spa with a lockable cover, positioned under a gazebo
- A porch attached to the rear of the house, including outdoor café blinds
- Paved drive-through side access leading to the garage and carport, allowing parking for up to six cars
- Drive-through garage with plumbing and power, featuring a kitchenette with heaps of storage - ideal potential for a teenage retreat
- A huge entertainer's pergola with café blinds all around, transforming the space into an additional outdoor room, perfect for large family BBQs and entertaining friends
- Breathtaking, well-established low-maintenance gardens and hedging
- Manicured front and back lawns
- Watering system throughout the front and back gardens
- Spotlights and sensor lighting to light up the night

Other features include:

- Updated skillion roof, with new bedding and pointing to the tiled roof
- Aluminium fascia
- External speakers and TV mounted under the pergola
- LED downlights throughout
- Two powerful split-system air conditioners plus one wall-mounted air conditioner
- Window locks to all windows and sliding glass doors

Homes like this are very rare. Be quick to get your copy of the contract and book yourself in for an inspection. Call Matt DeBono directly on 0432 443 770.


Disclaimer: We have been furnished with the above information; however, Harcourts Your Place St Marys and Plumpton gives no guarantees, undertakings or warnings concerning the accuracy, completeness or up-to-date nature of the information provided. All interested parties are responsible for their own independent inquiries to determine whether this information is in fact accurate.

SOLD BY JACK NAHAS & SINA TAFA
25A DUDLEY STREET, MOUNT DRUITT
Expertly crafted to deliver contemporary comfort, this brand-new freestanding duplex offers a free-flowing design, premium finishes and a versatile layout perfect for family living. With five bedrooms, dual living zones, seamless in/outdoor flow and a prized north-facing orientation, it promises a low-maintenance lifestyle in a high-growth setting. The property is located within a 900m stroll from Mt Druitt Station, Westfield and vibrant dining options.

- Free-flowing dual-level layout reveals a front lounge and an open living/dining zone
- Seamless transition to a private backyard, perfect for entertaining or alfresco dining
- Marble kitchen, waterfall edges, gas cooking, a dishwasher and steel appliances
- Five generous bedrooms include one on the ground floor, ideal as a guest room
- Master bedroom enjoys an ensuite that opens to a private balcony
- Fully tiled bathroom with black fixtures, a frameless shower and quality finishes
- Ducted air-conditioning, downlights, neutral tones and polished grey tiled flooring
- Lock-up garage with internal access, internal laundry and under-stair storage
- Positioned 550m from Mt Druitt Station, Westfield, schools, and library

** While we have provided the above information, Laing+Simmons makes no guarantees about its accuracy, completeness, or current relevance. All interested parties should conduct their own independent inquiries to verify the information.
